# Provisions

Each provision ID resolves here to its heading, owning page, scope, record layer, trace, strength, and verification mark, with the share of each page's rules a tool checks.

Run `litespec-maintain provisions` after any provision change. The repository validator fails when this page and the active standards disagree.

The active release states 1491 provisions in 4 groups across 5 areas.

## Standard

### core

| ID | Provision | Kind | Stack | Concern | Artifact | Layer | Trace | Strength | Mark | Evidence |
|:---|:---|:---|:---|:---|:---|:---|:---|:---|:---|:---|
| litespec/core/access#admit-the-system-identity-on-every-system-run-use-case | [Admit the System Identity on Every System-Run Use Case](../core/access.md#admit-the-system-identity-on-every-system-run-use-case) | rule | any | records | record:use-case, record:reaction, record:job | behavior | no | required | checkable | `litespec model check` reports `system-access-missing` for a reaction or job that runs, commands, or queries a use case whose access admits no system identity, and `record-invalid` for `runsAs` beside a `system` or `anonymous` grant. |
| litespec/core/access#authenticate-every-caller-through-a-declared-authentication-kind | [Authenticate Every Caller Through a Declared Authentication Kind](../core/access.md#authenticate-every-caller-through-a-declared-authentication-kind) | rule | any | records | record:entry-point | behavior | no | required | checkable | `litespec model check` reports `record-invalid` for an `authentication` kind outside session, token, device, link, app, and none. |
| litespec/core/access#authorize-every-use-case-through-a-declared-grant | [Authorize Every Use Case Through a Declared Grant](../core/access.md#authorize-every-use-case-through-a-declared-grant) | rule | any | records | record:use-case | behavior | no | required | checkable | `litespec model check` reports `record-invalid` for a grant outside permission, self, signed-in, anonymous, and system, and `reference-unknown` for a permission no record defines. |
| litespec/core/access#cite-the-setting-or-plan-behind-every-entitlement | [Cite the Setting or Plan Behind Every Entitlement](../core/access.md#cite-the-setting-or-plan-behind-every-entitlement) | rule | any | records | record:use-case | behavior | no | required | guidance | Does every entitlement grant cite its setting or plan? Check each grant beside the settings its use case cites. |
| litespec/core/access#declare-access-on-every-use-case | [Declare Access on Every Use Case](../core/access.md#declare-access-on-every-use-case) | rule | any | records | record:use-case | behavior | no | required | checkable | `litespec model check` reports `record-invalid` for a use case without `access`, `grant`, or `tenant`. |

| litespec/core/access#define-each-scope-level-in-the-glossary | [Define Each Scope Level in the Glossary](../core/access.md#define-each-scope-level-in-the-glossary) | rule | any | records | record:role | behavior | no | required | checkable | `litespec model check` reports `scope-term-missing` for a role scope with no glossary term titled with the level and Scope. |
